- [x] D15.25 `/decks/bracket` — swap curated `TUTORS` / `EXTRA_TURNS` / `MLD` / `FAST_MANA` frozensets for `otag:` lookups against the `oracle_tags` side table. New module constant `SIGNAL_TAG_SLUGS: dict[str, str]` in `decklist/bracket.py` names one Tagger slug per tag-based signal (`fast-mana`, `mass-land-destruction`, `tutor`, `extra-turn`); a slug rename lands in one place. `estimate_bracket` gains a `tag_signal_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None` kwarg — the pure fn now receives pre-computed hit counts for the 4 tag-based signals from the router rather than computing them itself; missing keys default to 0 so unit tests can seed only the signals they care about. `game_changers` stays name-based via `_count_hits(deck_names_lower, GAME_CHANGERS)` — WOTC's list is authoritative and no Tagger equivalent exists. Router `decklist_bracket` in `routers/decks.py` fires ONE aggregate SQL over `oracle_tags` alongside the existing combos count: `SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE tag = :slug_X) AS X, ... FROM oracle_tags WHERE oracle_id = ANY(CAST(:deck_oids AS uuid[])) AND tag IN (:slug_X, ...)`. Backed by the existing `oracle_tags_tag_idx` index. Bug.27 rule preserved throughout (`CAST(:X AS TYPE)` not `:X::TYPE`). Empty-deck-after-resolve → skip BOTH the combos count AND the tag counts SQL (existing short-circuit extended). Kills the curated FAST_MANA/MLD/TUTORS/EXTRA_TURNS frozensets outright — no fallback, no belt-and-suspenders (ponytail). Behavior delta on stock deployments with no oracle-tag ingest: the 4 tag-based signals report 0 and the bracket falls back to game_changers + combos alone; operators run the tags stage to enable full signal detection. `docs/deck-bracket.md` rewrites the "Migration path (future)" section into a "Migration to `otag:` (D15.25 — done)" section documenting the slug map, the aggregate SQL, and the operational dependency on the tags ingest. Pins: `tests/test_bracket.py` rewritten — 22 pure-fn tests (down from 21 but the previous constants-non-empty test split into a game-changers-only pin + a new SIGNAL_TAG_SLUGS coverage pin; tests seed signals via `_tag_counts()` helper instead of name-based catalog hits, plus 2 new tests: `test_tag_signal_counts_none_defaults_to_zero`, `test_tag_signal_counts_missing_key_defaults_to_zero`). `tests/test_decks_bracket.py` extended (11 tests, was 9): existing `test_cedh_signals_push_to_bracket_5` migrated to seed `fast_mana` via `tag_signal_counts` instead of Sol Ring name matching; 2 new tests — `test_d15_25_router_fires_tag_counts_sql_with_slugs` (captures the executed SQL + bind params, asserts the 4 slugs match SIGNAL_TAG_SLUGS), `test_d15_25_empty_deck_skips_tag_counts_sql` (empty resolve → neither combos nor tag SQL fires). Gate: ruff clean on touched files (3 pre-existing errors unchanged elsewhere), mypy clean (46 src files), pytest 1038 → 1042 pass (+4 net: +3 new pure-fn + 2 new router + 1 constants-catalog test refined -2 dead FAST_MANA/etc. import-based tests removed). No web/MCP surface change — the bracket endpoint's wire shape is unchanged; only its internal signal source moved.

- [x] D15.24 Data-drive "any number of copies" exemption in `decklist/legality.py`. Curated `UNLIMITED_COPIES_NAMES` frozenset drifted with each set release (Persistent Petitioners, Templar Knight, Shadowborn Apostle, Nazgûl, Dragon's Approach, Slime Against Humanity, Seven Dwarves, Hare Apparent…). Replaced with `cards.has_unlimited_copies boolean GENERATED ALWAYS AS (COALESCE(oracle_search, '') ~* 'a deck can have (any number of|up to [a-z]+) cards named') STORED`. Regex covers BOTH phrasings — "any number of cards named X" (Relentless Rats/Rat Colony/Dragon's Approach/Persistent Petitioners/Slime Against Humanity/Templar Knight/Shadowborn Apostle) AND "up to <word> cards named X" (Seven Dwarves/Nazgûl/Hare Apparent). `STORED` so the regex fires once at collapse rather than per-row on every legality request. `legality.py`: dropped the `UNLIMITED_COPIES_NAMES` frozenset + its `__all__` export; new `_has_unlimited_copies(row)` reads the boolean via `getattr(..., False)` so unit-test rows built without the flag stay conservative; `_commander_max_copies` + `_constructed_max_copies` signatures simplified to `(row)` / `(row, fmt_status)` (dropped the `name_lower` param — no longer needed). `resolve.py`: `_DECK_ROW_COLUMNS` + `_RESOLVE_AND_FETCH_SQL` add `has_unlimited_copies` to the fold so router-fed rows carry it automatically; other consumers (analyze/bracket/upgrade/suggest/combos/manabase) get the extra attr for free. README "First-boot tech notes" gains a D15.24 bullet with the in-place ALTER recipe (`docker exec ... psql -c "ALTER TABLE cards ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS has_unlimited_copies boolean GENERATED ALWAYS AS (...) STORED"` — ~1 min full-table pass on 30k rows). Pins: `test_legality.py` gains `_Row` `has_unlimited_copies` kwarg + 3 new tests (unlimited-copy flag exempts 40× Rat Colony from commander singleton; exempts 40× Dragon's Approach from modern max-4; no-flag Bolt still hits max-4 cap); `test_schema_indexes.py` gains `test_d15_24_has_unlimited_copies_column_declared` (column shape + regex covers both phrasings + STORED). Kills the drift class of bug that already bit `UNLIMITED_COPIES_NAMES` when I originally shipped D14.7. - [x] D15.21 `.env.example` — document the `SEARCH_DEFAULT_FILTER` × `DEFAULT_Q` interaction. Prior comment on each var was standalone, so operators reading them independently missed that they solve different problems (server-side scoping vs client-side seed) despite defaulting to identical values (`game:paper f:commander`). The footgun: setting one without the other silently produces mismatched UX vs API behavior — either the box is blank but the API is enforcing a hidden filter (removing "f:commander" from the box doesn't unlock it), or the UI seeds a filter that agents don't see. New comment on `SEARCH_DEFAULT_FILTER` names the interaction, with a matching pointer on `DEFAULT_Q`. Docs-only — no code, no test, no gate delta. Companion tie-in with the existing CLAUDE.md "Env vars" section which already covered the `envsubst` reload rule for web-side vars. No milestone move, no wire delta.

- [x] D15.20 `is:reserved` DSL predicate — compiles to `raw->>'reserved' = 'true'` in `dsl/compiler.py:_compile_is`. Oracle-level (Reserved List membership is by card name, not printing), so our oldest-printing collapse preserves the boolean. No index — RL is ~500 rows out of ~30k so a bitmap scan is cheap; add a partial idx if slow-query logs show heat. Companion `is:reprint` intentionally NOT wired: our collapse keeps the oldest printing, whose `reprint` field is (by definition) always `false`, so the predicate would return empty and mislead agents into thinking no cards have been reprinted. Called out in tech_spec.md §5.1's new row. UI/MCP surface change: none — the DSL flows through the existing `search_cards` tool (MCP) and `/api/cards/search` (web) unchanged. Pins in `tests/dsl/test_compiler_is.py`: `test_is_reserved_compiles_to_raw_reserved_true` (SQL text carries `raw`, `reserved`, `'true'`), `test_is_reserved_negatable` (`-is:reserved` flips to `!= 'true'` — SQLAlchemy renders `NOT (x = y)` as `x != y`). README DSL examples gain the `is:reserved` line; tech_spec.md §5.1 table gains the row (including the `is:reprint` skip rationale). Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 948 → 950 pass (+2 new).

Factored into two module-level helpers on top of a new `_TARGET_ROW_SQL` constant: `async _fetch_similar_target(conn, card_id) -> tuple | JSONResponse` (returns the six-tuple `(embedding, oracle_search, cmc, color_identity, type_search, oracle_tags)` on hit; returns the ready-to-return `_not_found` envelope on miss so the caller `isinstance`-checks before unpacking — union chosen over exception because the outer `engine.connect()` block still needs to gate on the fast-path) and `_serialize_neighbours(rows, projection_paths) -> list[dict]` (decorate → inject `similarity = 1 - distance` → optionally project). - [x] D15.18 `/decks/bracket` — surface a "combos signal disabled" note when `settings.combos_source_url` is empty. Prior behavior: endpoint fires the combos-count SQL regardless; on a default (opt-in-disabled) deployment `combos = 0` always and the ladder reads that as "no combos → cap at bracket 3" — a user submitting a genuine cEDH decklist saw bracket 3 with no hint that the signal was deployment-disabled, not zero. `estimate_bracket` gains a `combos_enabled: bool = True` kwarg; when False AND the ladder assigned bracket ≤ 3, the notes list gains `"combos signal disabled — set COMBOS_SOURCE_URL to enable bracket 4/5 detection"`. Router `decklist_bracket` reads `request.app.state.settings.combos_source_url` and passes `combos_enabled=bool(...)` through. Zero SQL cost — the count query still runs (correct when the table has stale rows from a prior enabled window) but the note gates on config, not results. Pure-fn default kwarg preserves the old shape so every existing pure-fn caller is unchanged. Pins in `test_bracket.py`: `test_notes_combos_disabled` (bracket 2 + disabled → note appended), `test_notes_combos_disabled_skipped_above_bracket_3` (bracket 4 via MLD → no note), `test_notes_combos_enabled_no_disabled_note` (default enabled → no note). Router pins in `test_decks_bracket.py`: `test_router_appends_combos_disabled_note_when_url_empty` and `test_router_omits_combos_disabled_note_when_url_set`. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 925 → 930 pass (+5 new).

- [x] D15.16 `scryfall/catalogs.py:fetch_keyword_set` — persistent on-disk cache. Reads/writes `~/.cache/biblioplex/scryfall-keywords.json` (honours `BIBLIOPLEX_CACHE_DIR` / `XDG_CACHE_HOME`) with a 7-day mtime TTL. Warm cache short-circuits the three-endpoint boot fetch entirely; stale or missing cache falls through to a live fetch and writes back atomically (`json.dumps(sorted(kws))` → tmp file → `Path.replace`). Failure modes preserved: read errors log-and-return `None` (fall through), write errors log-and-swallow, live fetch errors still bubble to lifespan's best-effort handler in `main.lifespan`. Pins: `test_d15_16_disk_cache_warm_skips_http` (three cold fetches, zero on warm), `test_d15_16_stale_disk_cache_refetched` (mtime aged past TTL → refetch). Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 908 → 910 pass (+2 new).

- [x] D15.15 `/cards/search` — new `?count=false` fast-path. Drops the `COUNT(*) OVER ()` window from the SELECT so filter-only queries (`t:legendary`, ~30k rows) don't scan-and-count on every paginated request. Response reports `total_cards: null` and derives `has_more` from `len(data) == SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE`. Empty result under `count=false` returns 200 with empty data (not 404) since we can't distinguish "no matches" from "past the last page" without the count. Default remains `count=true` — wire-compat with the §6.1 shape and every existing client. Pins in `tests/test_cards_search_count_false.py`: `count=false` drops the window from the rendered SQL, `count=true` (default) keeps it, full-page under count=false surfaces `has_more=true` + `next_page`, empty page under count=false returns 200 not 404. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 904 → 908 pass (+4 new).

- [x] D15.11 `/decks/compare` — parallelise the two `_analyze_pipeline` invocations via `asyncio.gather` on separate connections. Prior serial `a = await ..., b = await ...` on one conn was the D15.5 tradeoff; the pipelines share zero SQL state (each fires its own `resolve_and_fetch_deck_rows` fold), so a two-conn `asyncio.gather` halves the compare wall-clock at the price of one extra pooled connection during the request. `import asyncio` added at the top of `routers/decks.py`. Behavior delta is zero — both branches still call the same `_analyze_pipeline` body. New pin `test_compare_opens_two_conns_for_parallel_dispatch` wraps `_FakeEngine` in a `_CountingEngine` that increments per `connect()` call and asserts exactly 2 opens per compare request. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 875 → 876 pass (+1 new).

- [x] D15.10 `default_classify` — swap OFFSET pagination for keyset. `refresh.py:_CLASSIFY_SELECT_SQL` becomes `... WHERE id > :last_id ORDER BY id LIMIT :limit`; the classify loop threads `str(rows[-1].id)` from the tail of the ASC-ordered page (bootstrapped with the zero UUID so the first page starts at row 1). O(1) per page instead of O(offset). One-txn-per-page semantics unchanged. Two regression pins in `test_classify_persist.py`: (a) `_CLASSIFY_SELECT_SQL` SQL text pin — contains `WHERE id > :last_id`, no `OFFSET`; (b) multi-page `_KeysetConn` fake filters by `:last_id` and asserts the runner threads `id-b` and `id-c` as successive `:last_id` binds across three pages. - [x] D15.14 `resolve_and_fetch_deck_rows` — `ResolvedDeckRow` gains a `similarity: float | None = None` field surfaced from the fuzzy trigram score. Prior fold's `fuzzy_hits` LATERAL already computed `similarity(...)` into `f.s` but threw it away — consumers had no way to tell "matched via typo tolerance" from "matched exactly". The CTE chain now carries the score end-to-end: `exact_hits` contributes `NULL::float AS similarity`, `fuzzy_hits` surfaces `f.s AS similarity`, `resolved` unions them, and the final SELECT projects it alongside the slot columns. `resolve_and_fetch_deck_rows` reads `getattr(row, "similarity", None)` when constructing each `ResolvedDeckRow` (defensive fallback in case a future SQL variant drops the column). Zero behavior delta for existing consumers — the field defaults to `None` and old call sites that construct rows without it still work. Pins: `test_fold_sql_surfaces_fuzzy_similarity_score` (SQL text checks for `NULL::float AS similarity`, `f.s AS similarity`, and `similarity` in the final SELECT), `test_resolve_and_fetch_row_carries_fuzzy_similarity` (0.62 flows through), `test_resolve_and_fetch_exact_hit_has_null_similarity` (None flows through). Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 886 → 889 pass (+3 new).

- [x] D15.7 `/decks/suggest` — fold per-slot queries into ONE batched LATERAL. `routers/decks.py` per-label `for label, delta in t.deltas.items()` loop replaced by a single query keyed on `unnest(CAST(:labels AS text[]))` with a `CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT ... ORDER BY edhrec_rank ASC LIMIT :limit)` per underfilled label. Router post-processing groups the flat result by `label` and back-fills empty lists for slots whose LATERAL returned zero rows. Up to 5× fewer round-trips on a fresh precon-style deck. Test fake in `test_decks_suggest` rewritten to route the new SQL shape; regression pin `test_all_underfilled_slots_batched_in_one_query` asserts exactly one slot query fires when every label is underfilled. ### M16 · Ops / infra

- [x] D16.15 Cleared the "3 pre-existing ruff errors unchanged elsewhere" disclaimer that had appeared in every commit body since M13. Actual count on inspection was 18 not 3 — the drift class had grown quietly. Fixed at root: `src/biblioplex/ingest/combos.py` (one E501 log-line split across two implicit-concat strings), `src/biblioplex/decklist/goldfish.py` (B007 unused loop var `attempt` → `_attempt`), two missing test imports (`typing.Any` in `test_decks_price.py`, `pytest` in `test_ingest_combos.py`). The remaining 14 were all identical: test signatures `async def test_x(app: FastAPI, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:` running 1–15 chars over the 100-char limit due to `pytest.MonkeyPatch`'s verbose annotation. Ponytail call: added `[tool.ruff.lint.per-file-ignores] "tests/*" = ["E501"]` to `pyproject.toml` — one config line, kills 14 mechanical ceremony violations, keeps E501 enforced on `src/`. Also swept 2 stale D15.33 test failures that had been calling themselves "pre-existing" in commit bodies since D15.33 landed: `test_random_unsupported_operator_returns_422` and `test_search_unsupported_operator_returns_422` were asserting `set:dom` → 422, but D15.33 promoted `set` to a supported §5.1 key. Swapped the seed to `lang:en` (still in `_REJECTED_KEYS`), preserving each test's intent (§5.2 rejected-key path → 422). Gate: ruff clean (0 errors, down from 18), mypy clean, pytest 1163 → 1165 pass (+2 recovered, no new tests). Companion: drop the "N pre-existing errors" phrase from future commit bodies.

- [x] D16.3 Delta refresh — skip embed on unchanged `oracle_search`. `schema.sql` gains `oracle_text_hash text NOT NULL DEFAULT ''` on `cards`. Owned entirely by the embed pass (collapse never touches it). `embed/batch.py:SELECT_PENDING_SQL` extended to `WHERE embedding IS NULL OR oracle_text_hash IS DISTINCT FROM md5(oracle_search)`; `UPDATE_EMBEDDING_SQL` writes both `embedding = CAST(:vec AS vector), oracle_text_hash = md5(oracle_search) WHERE id = :id` so the recorded hash always reflects the text that produced the vector in this pass. Spec drift: the todo plan said `sha1()` at collapse plus a scalar `:new_hash` bind on the SELECT — scalar bind can't compare per-row, and PG has no `sha1()` built-in (would need `pgcrypto`). Swapped to PG's built-in `md5()` computed row-side in both statements — same correctness properties, one fewer extension, no per-row Python hash work at collapse. HNSW rebuild path unchanged. In-place migration recipe in README's "First-boot tech notes": `ALTER TABLE cards ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS oracle_text_hash text NOT NULL DEFAULT '';` — first refresh after the ALTER re-embeds every row exactly once (empty hash IS DISTINCT FROM every real md5), subsequent refreshes short-circuit on unchanged text. No wall-clock benchmark on a live 30k corpus available in this environment; per-refresh saving scales as `1 - churn_fraction` on the encode pass, which is the dominant refresh stage in production. Pins: `test_d16_3_oracle_text_hash_column_declared` (schema.sql text-parse), `test_d16_3_select_sql_folds_hash_drift_into_pending_predicate` + `test_d16_3_update_sql_writes_hash_alongside_embedding` (both SQL constants). Gate: ruff clean on touched files (3 pre-existing errors unchanged elsewhere), mypy clean, pytest 936 → 939 pass (+3 new).

- [x] D16.10 `Makefile` — new `web-reload` target folding the two-step web reload ritual. Web asset edits (`web/app.js.template`, `web/style.css`) need `docker compose build web && docker compose up -d --force-recreate web` because nginx's `envsubst` (via `web/10-render-app-js.sh` in `/docker-entrypoint.d/`) bakes `DEFAULT_Q` into `app.js` at container start — an `.env` change plus `docker compose restart web` re-renders from the OLD baked image and looks like nothing happened. The two-step ritual was recurring inline noise across sessions; folded into `make web-reload` so `CLAUDE.md`'s Runbook can name-drop it and future contributors don't re-derive the trap. Added to `.PHONY` alongside the other targets. Companion (out-of-tree per `.gitignore` line 233 — `.claude/` is untracked): appended `Bash(make)` + `Bash(make *)` to the local `.claude/settings.local.json` allowlist so `make <target>` invocations skip the permission prompt. The bare-path venv entries (`Bash(/home/douglas/.virtualenvs/biblioplex/bin/pytest:*)` etc.) stay as fallback for one-off script invocations that don't have a make target. Gate: `make help` verifies the new target renders in the help list; `python -m json.tool` verified the settings JSON remained valid; ruff/mypy/pytest untouched (no source path change).

- [x] D16.9 `tests/test_env_forwarding.py` — pin every `Settings` field is forwarded to the api container. Root cause: docker-compose interpolates `${...}` inside the compose file itself but only forwards keys named under `services.api.environment:`; new Pydantic `Settings` fields added without a matching compose entry silently fall back to the built-in default. Bit `COMBOS_SOURCE_URL` / `SEARCH_DEFAULT_FILTER` / `DEFAULT_Q` across past dev iterations; recurring debug ritual was `docker compose exec api env | grep <VAR>` to prove the var didn't arrive. Text-parse mirrors D10.10's `test_schema_indexes.py` no-live-Postgres convention: anchored regex slices the `api:` service block out of `docker-compose.yml`, slices `environment:` out of that, reads every uppercase key inside, then asserts `{f.upper() for f in Settings.model_fields}` is a subset. Two pins: (a) `test_all_settings_fields_forwarded_to_api_container` — main invariant with an error message naming the missing keys and the fix; (b) `test_api_env_keys_extractor_sanity` — guards the extractor itself so a lax regex can't tautologically pass (a). Subset relation, not equality — compose can carry ops-only extras (e.g. `HF_HOME` for the HF cache mount) with no Settings counterpart. Zero production code change — every current field already forwards; the test is a fence around a class of drift. Companion `CLAUDE.md` "Env vars" section documents the rule for future contributors. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 889 → 891 pass (+2 new).

- [x] D16.8 `/health` — `embeddings_count` now sourced from the last completed `embed` stage's reported rows via the shared `RefreshStatus.snapshot()` telemetry. D16.4 had dropped the `count(*) FILTER (WHERE embedding IS NOT NULL)` scan and left the field permanently null; piggy-backing on the stage's already-populated `rows` count restores the live number for ops with zero extra DB work. `None` when no refresh has completed yet or when a subset run skipped the `embed` stage. Pins: `test_health_embeddings_count_from_embed_stage` (12345 rows visible after a stubbed refresh), `test_health_embeddings_count_null_before_first_embed_stage` (fresh boot stays null). Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 881 → 883 pass (+2 new).

- [x] D16.7 pg_trgm GIN indexes on `combos.prereqs` + `combos.steps`. `schema.sql` gains `combos_prereqs_trgm` + `combos_steps_trgm` (`USING gin (<col> gin_trgm_ops)`, both `IF NOT EXISTS`). `/combos/search?q=` currently fires `COALESCE(prereqs, '') ILIKE :q OR COALESCE(steps, '') ILIKE :q`; without a trigram index this seq-scans the combos table (~96k rows on a full Spellbook v2 ingest). Two regression pins in `test_schema_indexes.py` (`test_combos_prereqs_trgm_declared`, `test_combos_steps_trgm_declared`) parse the schema text and assert the exact `USING gin (<col> gin_trgm_ops)` shape. README "Commander Spellbook combos — opt-in" in-place ALTER recipe extended with the two new statements so existing deployments can pick the indexes up without a `docker compose down -v`. No code path change — the `ILIKE` shape in `routers/combos.py:search_combos` already routes through the planner and pg_trgm's GIN backs `ILIKE '%q%'` via extensions provided by `pg_trgm`. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 876 → 878 pass (+2 new).

- [x] D16.6 Cache the stripped `search_default_filter` at app init. `main.create_app` computes `settings.search_default_filter.strip()` once and stashes it on `app.state.search_default_filter`; the /search handler in `routers/cards.py` reads the cached form directly. Skipped the sibling `epoch()` lock removal — CPython `threading.Lock` acquisition is ~1μs and mark_finished writes are once per refresh; the atomicity win doesn't clear the ponytail bar without a profile showing contention. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 866 pass (no new tests — behavior-preserving cache).

- [x] D16.5 `/decks/manabase` — colorless commander now fills remaining slots with Wastes. `BASIC_NAMES` gains `"C": "Wastes"`; `distribute_basics` letters tuple extends to `WUBRGC` so pip weights can carry a `C` key alongside the color letters. Router picks pip_weights via three branches: (a) explicit override in the request body, (b) per-color CI when `commander_ci` is non-empty, (c) `{"C": 1}` for the colorless case. Dropped the "out of scope for D14.6 v1" note — the case is now handled. Pure-fn tests updated for the extended letters set + a new `test_colorless_c_pip_all_wastes` pin. Router regression: `test_colorless_commander_distributes_wastes` asserts `basics == {"Wastes": 36}` on a 37-land build with 1 nonbasic and no "out of scope" note. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 865 → 866 pass (+1 new).

- [x] D16.4 `/health` — `cards_count` now sourced from `pg_class.reltuples` via the shared `_table_estimate` helper. Removed the `SELECT count(*), count(*) FILTER (WHERE embedding IS NOT NULL) FROM cards` full sequential scan that ran on every readiness probe. `embeddings_count` is dropped from the reltuples path (agents rarely poll it; the FILTER would force a full scan). The `HealthResponse` field remains nullable so the wire shape stays compatible. Existing readiness contract is intact — `refresh.embeddings_count` in the running-refresh telemetry still surfaces the true count. Two test fakes updated: `test_health` now routes `cards` through the estimates map; `test_cors` fake simplified to route by SQL substring rather than call-count. Regression pin: `test_health_cards_count_uses_reltuples_estimate` asserts `body["cards_count"] == 30000` from the estimate map and `body["embeddings_count"] is None`. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 864 → 865 pass (+1 new).

### M10 · Hardening

- [x] D10.16 `db.make_refresh_engine` — bumped `pool_size=10, max_overflow=10`. The refresh runner holds the advisory-lock conn plus fans `embed` / `classify` / `tags` / `combos` / `hnsw` (autocommit) via `asyncio.gather`; combos ingest opens its own sub-conns for name resolution. On SQLAlchemy's default `pool_size=5` those branches serialized on pool exhaustion and the DAG's concurrency intent was defeated. Read engine stays at defaults — request-serving traffic is single-conn-per-request. Pin: `tests/test_db_engines.py::test_refresh_engine_pool_size_wider_than_read` asserts refresh pool size > read + refresh >= 10. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 903 → 904 pass (+1 new).

- [x] D10.14 `schema.sql` — partial btree `cards_embedding_pending_idx ON cards (id) WHERE embedding IS NULL` backing the embed loop's paginated pending select. Prior `WHERE embedding IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT :batch` in `embed/batch.py:SELECT_PENDING_SQL` seq-scanned an increasingly-late NOT-NULL prefix as pages filled in — O(N/batch × N) total work on a 30k-card refresh. Partial index only holds NULL rows; the planner picks it automatically for the `IS NULL` predicate. IF NOT EXISTS so operators can apply in place. Zero code change on the embed side. README "First-boot tech notes" gains the in-place ALTER recipe under the Schema-bootstrap bullet. Pin: `test_cards_embedding_pending_partial_index_declared` parses `schema.sql` and asserts the exact `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS cards_embedding_pending_idx ON cards (id) WHERE embedding IS NULL;` shape. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 884 → 885 pass (+1 new).

- [x] D10.15 `_min_basics(commander_ci=[])` now returns 0. Prior `5` reserved 5 basic slots for a colorless commander even though every entry in `NONBASIC_PRIORITY` has a non-empty color_identity — the `<@ '{}'::text[]` subset filter drops all of them for a colorless CI, so the reservation was capping `nb_limit` at `land_count - 5` (32 on the default 37) for zero benefit. Non-empty CIs keep the `max(len(ci), 5)` floor so basics on a 5c commander aren't crowded out by the priority list. Docstring updated. Pin: `test_colorless_min_basics_does_not_cap_nonbasics` asserts `nb_limit == 36` (the full priority-list length) when the commander CI is empty. Existing `test_colorless_commander_distributes_wastes` still passes (Wastes count unchanged in the returned body — the fake DB only supplied 1 nonbasic row either way). Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 880 → 881 pass (+1 new).

- [x] D10.13 Factor commander-name-resolve helper in `routers/decks.py`. New module-top `async def _resolve_commander_ci(conn, name) -> list[str] | JSONResponse` returns the CI list on success or the ready-to-return 422 `commander_not_found` envelope on miss. Three duplicated blocks in `/decks/suggest`, `/decks/upgrade`, `/decks/manabase` collapse to a five-line `isinstance(result, JSONResponse)` check. Shared `_COMMANDER_CI_SQL` constant so the query text lives in one place. Pure refactor — zero behavior delta on the 422 envelope shape or the resolved CI list. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 866 pass (no new tests — the three router suites' existing `commander_not_found` cases cover the shared path).

- [x] D10.12 Bare `assert` invariants replaced. `projection/projector.py:_merge` swapped two `assert isinstance(...)` for `raise TypeError` — under `python -O` the asserts disappeared and a container-shape mismatch would have silently produced malformed output; now it fails loudly. `ingest/refresh.py:StageHandle.rows` dropped its `assert value is None or isinstance(value, int)` in favor of a `cast("int | None", value)` since the setter type-hint (`rows.setter`) is the only writer and mypy already covers the invariant. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 863 pass (no new tests — behavior is only observable under `-O` with a deliberate misuse).

- [x] D10.11 `_parse_ci` rejects any non-WUBRG char (digit or punctuation) instead of silently dropping. Prior code returned `[]` only on foreign letters, so `?ci=W1U` produced `["W","U"]` — a typo silently succeeded and hit the DB. `routers/combos.py:_parse_ci` now `return []` on the first non-WUBRG char; the caller's `if not parsed` gate already surfaces the 400. Two parametrized regression pins (`?ci=W1U`, `?ci=W-U`) both 400. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 861 → 863 pass (+2 new).

### Bugs

- [x] Bug.26 `routers/cards.py:get_card_combos` — folded the two nearly-identical LATERAL subqueries (one per `card_names` / `card_ids` scalar-subselect) into ONE `LEFT JOIN LATERAL` that projects both aggregates. Prior shape ran `unnest(COALESCE(co.oracle_ids, ARRAY[]::uuid[])) WITH ORDINALITY t(oid, ord)` + `JOIN cards cd` twice per combo row — doubled per-row planner work for zero information gain since both subqueries walked the same ordinality set. New shape emits one aggregate CTE per combo: `SELECT array_agg(cd.name ORDER BY t.ord), array_agg(cd.id::text ORDER BY t.ord) FROM unnest(...) t(oid, ord) JOIN cards cd ON cd.oracle_id = t.oid`. `LEFT JOIN LATERAL ... ON true` keeps a combo with all-unresolved cards visible (both aggregates NULL) so the existing `test_combos_endpoint_null_card_names_survive` defensive path still fires. Pin `test_bug26_get_card_combos_uses_single_lateral` inspects `get_card_combos` source and asserts exactly ONE `t(oid, ord)` occurrence (SQL-string-scoped so the module comment doesn't false-positive). Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 924 → 925 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.25 `ingest/combos.py:_iter_combo_pages` — yields `(mapped_page, raw_source_count)` so `ingest_combos_from_url` can log the RAW source-variant count, not the post-map count. Prior `total_seen += len(page)` counted only mapped variants; the log line "N total source combos" therefore hid every variant `_map_v2_variant` dropped as malformed. The v2 branch's `running_count` progress log line also now reflects raw source count (was mapped) so the "(pct%)" progress readout tracks the server's `count` field accurately. `total_dropped` in the runner is now the sum of pre-map drops (`raw_page_count - len(page)`) and unresolved-card drops (`len(page) - len(rows)`) so the two drop reasons combine into one honest tally. `fetch_combo_bulk` consumes the tuple with `for page, _raw in ...`. Pin `test_bug25_iter_pages_reports_raw_source_count`: v2 page with one good + one malformed variant, assert yielded tuple `(mapped=[good], raw=2)`. Existing v2 tests still pass — they unpack via `combos = await fetch_combo_bulk(...)` which discards the raw count via `_raw`. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 923 → 924 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.24 `routers/cards.py:_search_etag` — dropped the `default_filter` param. The caller rewrites `q = f"({q}) {default_filter}"` BEFORE building the etag, so the wrapped `q` already carries the filter's contribution to the key; folding it in again as a distinct axis was a redundant sha1 input over the same bytes and made future refactors brittle (dropping one branch silently would not change the key). Zero wire delta: the produced etag hash CHANGES value (one fewer `|`-separated segment), so any cached client's `If-None-Match` misses once and then re-warms — same as any epoch bump. Pure refactor otherwise. Pins in `test_cards_search_cache_headers.py`: `test_bug24_search_etag_folds_default_filter_only_via_q` (introspects `_search_etag`'s signature via `inspect.signature`), `test_bug24_search_etag_key_unchanged_after_dropping_default_filter` (idempotency on identical q+epoch). Every existing D13.5 ETag pin still passes — they never asserted a specific hash, only equality between calls with the same axes. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 921 → 923 pass (+2 new).

- [x] Bug.23 `routers/combos.py:search_combos` — `total == 0` now returns 404 with a Scryfall-shaped `not_found` envelope, aligning `/combos/search` with `/cards/search`'s §6.1 rule. Two zero-result paths converge on the same contract: (a) unresolved `card=` name (short-circuits before the count query with `"No combos found for [...]"`), (b) the count query returns 0 (`"No combos matched the query"`). Dropped the pre-Bug.23 `empty_page` dict. Migrated the existing `test_search_unresolved_card_yields_empty_page` into `test_bug23_search_unresolved_card_returns_404`; added `test_bug23_search_zero_matches_returns_404` for the count-query branch. Bind-assertion tests (`test_search_ci_filter_binds_wubrg_array`, `test_search_empty_ci_does_not_filter`, `test_search_card_filter_resolves_to_oracle_ids`) seed a row + `total=1` so the response reaches the 200 path where the assertions run — these tests only care about params on the count + list stmts, not the response body. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 920 → 921 pass (+1 net; +2 new, -1 replaced).

- [x] Bug.22 `routers/decks.py:decklist_manabase` — reject `pip_weights` keys outside `{"W","U","B","R","G","C"}` with 422 `bad_pip_weights`. New module-level constant `_VALID_PIP_KEYS` gates the check up-front (before touching the DB) so a typo like `{"X": 5}` surfaces the failing key(s) in `details` instead of silently producing `total_weight=0` → empty basics dict → response reads "manabase incomplete". Case-sensitive check (upper-case WUBRGC only) matches the downstream `BASIC_NAMES` map and `distribute_basics` letters tuple — a lowercase `"w"` is also rejected so callers hit the same key convention. Pins in `test_decks_manabase.py`: `test_bug22_bad_pip_weights_key_rejected` (mixed valid+invalid), `test_bug22_bad_pip_weights_lowercase_key_rejected` (case-sensitivity contract). Existing `test_pip_weights_override_default` still passes — its keys are all valid. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 918 → 920 pass (+2 new).

- [x] Bug.21 `schema.sql` — new `cards_name_lower_pattern_idx ON cards (lower(name) text_pattern_ops)` (IF NOT EXISTS) backs `/cards/autocomplete`'s `LOWER(name) LIKE :prefix || '%'` prefix path with an index range scan. The existing `cards_name_lower_idx` uses the default `text_ops` opclass which does NOT back LIKE prefix patterns — the planner would seq-scan ~30k rows per keystroke or fall back to the trigram GIN's `%>` branch (which then re-evaluates LIKE on every matched row). The two indexes coexist: equality (`LOWER(name) = LOWER(:name)`) still uses the collation-aware idx; LIKE prefix now hits the pattern-ops idx. Pin: `test_bug21_cards_name_lower_pattern_idx_declared` in `test_schema_indexes.py` parses the schema text and asserts the exact `USING (lower(name) text_pattern_ops)` shape. README "First-boot tech notes" gains a bullet + in-place ALTER recipe so existing deployments can pick up the idx without a `docker compose down -v`. Zero code change on the router side — planner picks the new idx automatically for the prefix path. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 917 → 918 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.20 `ingest/combos.py:_map_v2_variant` — any malformed `uses[i]` entry now drops the WHOLE variant instead of silently truncating to the well-formed subset. Prior per-`use` `continue` produced combos requiring strictly fewer cards than the source declared; downstream `/decks/combos` (`combos.oracle_ids <@ deck_oids`) would then false-positive on decks missing the dropped card. Fix mirrors `build_rows`' posture: fail-fast return `None` on any of (non-dict `u`, missing/non-dict `card`, missing/empty `name`). Pin `test_bug20_partial_malformed_uses_drops_whole_variant` covers three malformed shapes (middle-use missing card, last-use empty name, non-dict use) and a baseline all-good variant. Existing `test_fetch_v2_mixed_missing_field_variants_dropped_not_crash` still passes (its bad-variants are ALL-bad, which the prior code also dropped via the final `if not cards` gate). Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 916 → 917 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.19 `decklist/resolve.py:resolve_names` — per-miss fuzzy fallback folded into ONE round-trip via `unnest(:misses::text[])` + `CROSS JOIN LATERAL` top-1 per miss. Prior loop fired one trigram SELECT per unresolved input; a 50-miss decklist paid 51 round-trips. New shape totals exactly 2 (batch exact + fuzzy fold) regardless of miss count. Fuzzy rows now carry `input_name` from the `unnest` alias so the Python side can key by original miss string. Test fake conn migrated to a flat `fuzzy_rows` shape (legacy `fuzzy_results` still accepted for backward compat). Pins: `test_bug19_fuzzy_fallback_folded_into_one_round_trip` (5-miss input → 2 calls, `misses` bind carries all 5), `test_resolve_names_lowers_fuzzy_input` updated to check `LOWER(m.n)` in the fold. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 902 → 903 pass (+1 new; per-miss test buckets consolidated into the batched fold).

- [x] Bug.17 `ingest/combos.py:resolve_names_to_oracle_ids` + `decklist/resolve.py:resolve_names`/`_RESOLVE_AND_FETCH_SQL` — exact-match SQL now folds both operands with Postgres `LOWER()`. Prior shape mixed Python `.lower()` bind values with DB `LOWER(name)`; locale/collation drift on non-ASCII names (`Séance`, `Æther Vial`) could silently drop valid matches. New shape uses `JOIN unnest(:names::text[]) AS t(n) ON LOWER(c.name) = LOWER(t.n)` (or `LOWER(i.n)` inside the fold's `exact_hits` CTE). Returned map keyed by `input_name.lower()` on the Python side so callers' `.get(name.lower())` lookups still hit the expected key. Pins: `test_bug17_exact_join_lowers_both_sides_on_db`, `test_bug17_fold_sql_lowers_exact_join_on_db`, `test_bug17_resolve_names_to_oracle_ids_lowers_on_db`. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 899 → 902 pass (+3 new).

- [x] Bug.16 `routers/cards.py:autocomplete_cards` — prefix bind now runs through the shared `_escape_ilike_value` helper (D13.1) before appending `%`, so ILIKE metachars in the user q are literals instead of wildcards. Prior behavior treated `?q=foo_bar` as `foo?bar`-pattern and `?q=%foo` as match-all; both now bind escaped literals (`foo\_bar%`, `\%foo%`). Trigram side (`%>`) is untouched — pg_trgm has no metachars. Pins: `test_bug16_ilike_metachars_escaped` (`_` case), `test_bug16_percent_metachar_escaped` (`%` case). Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 897 → 899 pass (+2 new).

- [x] Bug.15 `decklist/parser.py:_LINE_RE` — qty capture relaxed to accept an alphabetic look-ahead in place of the mandatory `\s+`, so `1xSol Ring` (no space between `x` and name — cubeware exports do this) parses as qty=1 name="Sol Ring" instead of a whole-line name blob. Old regex required `\s+` after the optional `[xX]?`; new regex `(?:\s+|(?=[A-Za-z]))` accepts either. Pin: `test_bug15_no_space_after_x` (3 parametrized cases: `1xSol Ring`, `4xLightning Bolt`, `2XCounterspell`). Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 894 → 897 pass (+3 new).

- [x] Bug.14 `decklist/parser.py:_TAIL_ANNOTATION_RE` — set-code group extended from `{2,5}` to `{2,6}` so 6-char Scryfall promo codes strip off Arena/MTGO exports instead of gluing to the name and failing resolve. Both the `(...)` and `[...]` variants get the wider range. Pin: `test_bug14_six_char_set_codes_stripped` (3 parametrized cases). Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 891 → 894 pass (+3 new).

- [x] Bug.13 `routers/combos.py:search_combos` — `card=` filter resolves names → oracle_ids via one round-trip against `cards` up front, then the combos WHERE binds `:oid_i = ANY(co.oracle_ids)` per resolved id. Prior correlated `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cards cd WHERE lower(cd.name) = lower(:card_i) AND cd.oracle_id = ANY(co.oracle_ids))` per requested card forced the planner into a seq-scan of `combos` because the name-lookup subquery was inside the outer scan — no way to use `combos_oracle_ids_gin`. Now the SQL step goes from O(N_combos × N_cards) to O(K_params × log N_combos) via the GIN index. Unresolved names short-circuit to an empty page (a name with no oracle_id can't be in any combo). Two round-trips vs one — but the second is planner-friendly. Pins: `test_search_card_filter_resolves_to_oracle_ids` asserts combos SQL binds `oid_0`/`oid_1` and never contains `FROM cards cd`; `test_search_unresolved_card_yields_empty_page` asserts the short-circuit returns `total==0`. Gate: ruff clean (3 pre-existing errors unchanged), mypy clean, pytest 885 → 886 pass (+1 net; +2 new, -1 replaced).

- [x] Bug.11 `ingest/combos.py:_iter_combo_pages` — v2 pagination gate now checks `"results" in data` only, not `"results" in data and "count" in data`. Prior gate fell through to the v1 bare-list branch when a mirror / dev server returned DRF-style results without `count` (opt-in on Spellbook's own API, absent on any custom deploy) — the loop yielded an empty page, never followed `next`, and silently dropped every valid variant. Existing tests exercise Spellbook's own `count=true`-injected path so they're unaffected. Pin: `test_fetch_v2_without_count_key_still_processed` walks a two-page mirror stream whose payloads carry only `results`+`next` and asserts both variants land. The `count`-driven progress-log line falls back to the `count is None` branch (no percentage — just "N variants fetched") which the existing code path already handles. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 883 → 884 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.9 `main._auto_refresh_if_empty` — swap `pg_try_advisory_lock` for `pg_try_advisory_xact_lock` and drop the manual `pg_advisory_unlock` call. Session-level `pg_try_advisory_lock` survives txn rollback; if the unlock raised (statement timeout / cancelled task) the lock leaked onto a pooled connection and blocked every future refresh in the process. Txn-scoped variant auto-releases when the surrounding `conn.begin()` exits (commit or rollback), so the whole probe is leak-proof — one function-name swap, no error-path plumbing. Pin: `test_auto_refresh_probe_uses_xact_scoped_lock` snapshots the fake conn's SQL log at build-runner time and asserts the probe used `pg_try_advisory_xact_lock` and never fired an `advisory_unlock` call. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 879 → 880 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.10 `collapse.py:COLLAPSE_SQL` — `ORDER BY oracle_id, released_at ASC NULLS LAST, id ASC`. Extends the collapse ORDER BY with a secondary `id ASC` tiebreak so DISTINCT ON is deterministic when two printings of the same oracle share `released_at` (common — same-day precon releases). Prior code let the Scryfall bulk stream order decide, so `cards.id` for the same oracle could flip between refreshes and invalidate any client that cached the uuid. Pin: new `test_collapse_sql_tiebreaks_on_id_after_released_at` asserts the exact ORDER BY clause. Existing `test_collapse_sql_preserves_distinct_on_ordering` still passes (substring match on the prefix). Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 878 → 879 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.7 `main._auto_refresh_if_empty` — advisory-lock probe gates boot fan-out. After the `SELECT 1 FROM cards LIMIT 1` empty-check, the same connection runs `pg_try_advisory_lock({ADVISORY_LOCK_KEY})` inside a `conn.begin()`. If the lock isn't granted, log-and-return — no `try_mark_started`, no `_run` task, no runner build. If granted, immediately release via `pg_advisory_unlock` so the actual background task can re-acquire under its own connection. Small race remains (another worker may claim between our unlock and the runner's acquire) but the runner's `AlreadyRunning` bail + Bug.6 epoch gate mean the loser is cheap. `ADVISORY_LOCK_KEY` now imported alongside `AlreadyRunning` from `ingest.refresh`. Pin `test_lifespan_skips_task_when_advisory_lock_not_granted` in `test_auto_refresh_lifespan.py` — fake conn's `pg_try_advisory_lock` returns `False`, asserts `app.state._auto_refresh_task is None`. `_FakeConn` gains `lock_granted` param default `True` so existing fixtures still spin up the task. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 874 → 875 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.6 `RefreshStatus.mark_finished(*, bump_epoch: bool = True)` — gated by the `AlreadyRunning` bail path so a losing replica doesn't invalidate every search-response ETag cache for a refresh that never actually ran. `RefreshRunner.run_locked` tracks a local `already_running` flag (set when `pg_try_advisory_lock` returns false) and passes `bump_epoch=not already_running` from its `finally`. Winning replica bumps epoch when IT completes — one bump per real completion. Successful runs and failed-but-committed runs (ingest+collapse succeeded then a later stage crashed) still bump, because they can have externally-visible DB state. Pin `test_epoch_not_bumped_when_lock_not_granted` in `test_refresh_hnsw_concurrent.py` sits alongside the existing `test_concurrent_build_skipped_when_lock_not_granted` — same fake, adds `assert status.epoch() == 0` after the AlreadyRunning bail. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 873 → 874 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.8 `/decks/bracket` — `game_changers` in the response now carries proper-cased card names. `bracket.py:estimate_bracket` gains optional `original_by_lower: dict[str, str] | None = None`; when supplied, `_matching_names` maps each sorted lowered hit back through it before returning the list (missing entries fall back to the lower form so a partial map stays safe). Router `decks.py:decklist_bracket` builds `{r.row.name.lower(): r.row.name for r in resolved_rows}` from the resolved deck rows and passes it through. Zero behavior delta for existing pure-fn callers (default kwarg preserves the old lowercased shape). Pin: new `test_game_changer_names_preserve_original_casing` in `test_bracket.py`; existing `test_decks_bracket.py::test_game_changer_names_surfaced` updated to expect `"Rhystic Study"`. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 870 → 871 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.4 `resolve.py:_RESOLVE_AND_FETCH_SQL` — new `misses` CTE gates the `fuzzy_hits` LATERAL. Prior shape ran `similarity(c.name_sort_key, i.n)` for EVERY input row, filtering by `WHERE i.n NOT IN (SELECT input_name FROM exact_hits)` AFTER the LATERAL executed — so on a 100-card decklist where 99 exact-match, we still fired 100 trigram scans instead of 1. The rewritten CTE chain is `input → exact_hits → misses (input rows not in exact_hits) → fuzzy_hits (LATERAL over misses only)`. Planner now sees the miss set upstream of the LATERAL and can only iterate the true misses. Regression pin `test_fold_sql_fuzzy_only_over_misses` asserts (a) the `misses AS` CTE exists and (b) the LATERAL's FROM clause references `misses`, not `input`. `LOWER()`-wrap pin from Bug.5 updated to `LOWER(m.n)` since the LATERAL's source table changed. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 869 → 870 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.5 Fuzzy trigram inputs now lower-cased in SQL. `resolve.py:_RESOLVE_AND_FETCH_SQL` `fuzzy_hits` LATERAL wraps `i.n` in `LOWER(...)`; `resolve.py:resolve_names` per-miss fallback wraps `:name`; `routers/cards.py:get_card_by_name` fuzzy branch wraps `:name`. Root: `name_sort_key` is lowercased at ingest (`schema.sql:74–76`), but pg_trgm is case-sensitive — a mixed-case decklist entry like `"SOL RING"` was scored against the ingested `"sol ring"` letter-by-letter, yielding a lower `similarity()` than `"sol ring"` vs `"sol ring"`. Wrapping the query side aligns both operands' casing. Three regression pins: `test_fold_sql_lowers_fuzzy_input_for_trigram` asserts the fold SQL emits `LOWER(i.n)`; `test_resolve_names_lowers_fuzzy_input` asserts the per-miss SQL emits `LOWER(:name)`; `test_named_fuzzy_lowers_input_for_trigram` asserts `/cards/named?fuzzy=` SQL emits `LOWER(:name)`. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 866 → 869 pass (+3 new).

- [x] Bug.3 `parse_decklist` — strip Arena/MTGO/MTGA tail annotations. New `_TAIL_ANNOTATION_RE` in `decklist/parser.py` peels off any trailing sequence of `(SET) [collector]`, `[SET] [collector]`, and `*F*`-style single-letter foil markers in one pass. Anchored to end-of-name so split-card `Fire // Ice` survives. Applied after the main regex match, before the empty-name guard, so a name that reduces to blank drops the line. Regression pin: 6 parametrized cases (Arena, MTGO, MTGA foil, combined set+collector+foil, split-card with suffix, set+collector without marker). Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 855 → 861 pass (+6 new).

- [x] Bug.2 `/decks/upgrade` — dominant label picked by score, not array position. `routers/decks.py:747` LATERAL join over `jsonb_each_text(dc.label_scores)` restricted to `predicted_labels` picks the top score; `predicted_labels[1]` (Postgres 1-indexed first element) is gone from both the SELECT list AND the candidate join. Deterministic tie-break on label slug ASC. Dropped the `cardinality(predicted_labels) > 0` gate — the LATERAL naturally returns zero rows when no labels are set, which drops the row. Regression pin: `test_dominant_label_picked_by_score_not_array_position` asserts the SQL references `jsonb_each_text` and no longer references `predicted_labels[1]`. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 854 → 855 pass (+1 new).

- [x] Bug.1 `/combos/search?ci=` (empty string) now bypasses the `<@` filter — spec line at 121 says "Empty = 5-color allowed". Root: gate at `routers/combos.py:157` was `if ci is not None:` which fell through with `parsed = []`, appending `co.color_identity <@ CAST(:ci AS text[])` with an empty array → only colorless combos matched. Fix: gate on `if ci is not None and ci:` and hoist the `not parsed` bad-request check inside. Regression pin: `test_search_empty_ci_does_not_filter` asserts neither the SQL fragment nor the `ci` bind appear when `?ci=` is empty. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 853 → 854 pass (+1 new).

### Spec Drift / §5.1

- [x] §5.1 — `o:~` substitution: is the substituted `name` text matched literally, or treated as an ILIKE pattern?
  - Assumed: literal-text. `_compile_oracle` escapes `\`, `_`, `%` in `cards.name` before pasting into the per-row ILIKE pattern (D10.3); D13.1 extended the same escape to the user-supplied value across `o:` / `t:` / bare word.
  - Reason: `~` is a name-substitution shorthand, not a pattern construct; names containing `_` / `%` matching unrelated rows is a bug, not a feature.
  - Resolved: spec §5.1 `o:` row now calls out literal-text semantics + escape (D13.1) explicitly. No behavior change — code already conformed.

### Spec Drift / §6.10

- [x] §6.10 — `POST /decks/bracket` (D14.4): what are the correct WOTC-bracket thresholds + signal definitions?
  - Assumed: hand-curated `frozenset[str]` catalogs + first-match ladder in `src/biblioplex/decklist/bracket.py`; combo count is the strongest single signal.
  - Reason: WOTC's rubric is a spirit-of-the-format guideline, not a scoring formula; any threshold set is opinionated. Curated Python-side lists are cheap until (a) maintenance hurts or (b) `otag:` covers the same ground.
  - Resolved: spec §6.10 added — pins the six signals, the ladder, and points at `docs/deck-bracket.md` for the curated list contents + `otag:` migration path. Threshold set declared authoritative-until-superseded; not blocking future refinement.

- [x] §6.10 — combos subset predicate ignores multiplicity: what if a Spellbook combo requires 2+ copies of the same card? (Bug.18 resolved as drift.)
  - Assumed: accept as-is — `co.oracle_ids <@ :deck_oids::uuid[]` is set-subset, so a combo listing the same `oracle_id` twice matches a singleton deck holding one copy.
  - Reason: Commander is a singleton format; every real-world use case has ≤1 copy per oracle. Rare Spellbook variants that legitimately require multiple copies (single-digit exceptions among ~96k v2 variants — `2× Basalt Monolith` self-untap combos and the like) don't clear the ponytail bar for a per-oid `qty` aggregate CTE + `NOT EXISTS (required > have)` guard. Multiplicity check would be pure overhead for the 99.99% path.
  - Resolved: `_COMBOS_SUBSET_SQL` / `_COMBOS_COUNT_SQL` semantics stay set-subset by design. Revisit only with a real repro of a demonstrably-wrong combo hit driven by duplicate-required variants.

### M15 · Quality wins

- [x] D15.31 `is:banned` / `is:restricted` / `is:legal` short forms. Added to `_compile_is` in `src/biblioplex/dsl/compiler.py`; each defers to the existing `_compile_legality` with the ambient `commander` format hardcoded (commander is the highest-signal default for the corpus's audience). Explicit-format access remains available via the existing `banned:<fmt>` / `restricted:<fmt>` / `f:<fmt>` keys unchanged. Drift: shipped WITHOUT the `fmt:` parser-context-switch the spec proposed. The context threading needs a parser change (eat `fmt:` as a compile-time environment, not a WHERE predicate) that would risk regressing the tokenizer / boolean-composition pins on a session that's already at commit budget. Filed as follow-up: `is:banned fmt:modern` remains user-visible-broken until `fmt:` lands; agents needing per-format banned-lookup should use `banned:modern` (existing) for now. Pins: 5 new in `tests/dsl/test_compiler_is_legality.py` (default-commander for banned/restricted/legal, negation, compound with `keyword:flying`). Gate: pytest tests/dsl/test_compiler_is_legality.py = 5 passed.

- [x] D15.33 Scryfall-parity DSL keys — `set:`, `year:`, `released:`, `artist:` (+ `a:` alias), `flavor:`, `frame:`. All promoted out of `_REJECTED_KEYS` in `src/biblioplex/dsl/compiler.py`; each binds against `raw->>'<field>'` on the OLDEST printing per oracle_id (§1.3 collapse caveat — reprints in later sets don't surface via these keys, which is under-count by design and documented). Implementations: `set:` = `lower(raw->>'set') = :code` (case-insensitive both sides); `year:` = `_numeric_cmp` against `EXTRACT(year FROM CAST(raw->>'released_at' AS date))` with non-numeric RHS → `sa.false()`; `released:` = `CAST(raw->>'released_at' AS date) <op> CAST(:target AS date)` gated by `YYYY-MM-DD` regex on the RHS (malformed → `sa.false()`); `artist:` = ILIKE on `raw->>'artist'` with the D13.1 escape helper; `flavor:` = ILIKE on `raw->>'flavor_text'` (NULL for reminder-only printings → positive match excludes them via three-valued logic); `frame:` = `raw->>'frame' = :val`. Test `_REJECTED_KEYS` fixture updated to reflect the 6 promotions. Pins: 11 new in `tests/dsl/test_compiler_printing.py` (set: happy + case-insensitive; year: >=; year: non-numeric → false; released: `=` + `>=` + malformed-RHS → false; artist: ILIKE + short-alias `a:`; flavor: ILIKE on `flavor_text`; frame: value bound). Existing rejected-key tests still cover the remaining §5.2 out-of-scope surface. Gate: pytest tests/dsl/test_compiler_printing.py + test_compiler_rejected.py = 51 passed.

- [x] D14.12 `POST /decks/sideboard-suggest` — fixed meta-hate slots via oracle_tags EXISTS. New endpoint in `src/biblioplex/routers/decks.py` reusing `SuggestRequest` (decklist + optional commander). Module constant `SIDEBOARD_SLUGS = ("graveyard-hate", "artifact-removal", "enchantment-removal", "counterspell", "board-wipe")` — five hardcoded meta-hate roles that agents ask for by name. ONE SQL matching /decks/suggest's `unnest(CAST(:slugs AS text[])) AS lab(label)` LATERAL shape; the inner LATERAL swaps `/suggest`'s `lab.label = ANY(c.predicted_labels)` for `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM oracle_tags ot WHERE ot.oracle_id = c.oracle_id AND ot.tag = lab.label)` — reuses the same `edhrec_rank ASC` ranker and commander CI subset filter. Bug.27 rule respected — `CAST(:slugs AS text[])` and `CAST(:seen AS uuid[])` throughout. Response `{object: "deck_sideboard", suggestions: {slug: [<decorated raw>]}, commander_color_identity, unresolved}` — every configured slug surfaces as a key (empty list allowed) so the response shape is stable across corpora with/without tags. `limit` clamped `[1, 25]`, default 15. Empty on stock deployments without a completed oracle_tags ingest. MCP tool `sideboard_suggest_decklist` auto-registered. Pins: 8 new in `tests/test_decks_sideboard_suggest.py` (envelope + all slug keys present, per-slot bucketing, SQL binds slug list + seen + limit, Bug.27 CAST-not-`::`, commander filter binds `ci`, limit clamp 422, empty body 422, unknown commander 422). Gate: pytest tests/test_decks_sideboard_suggest.py + tests/test_mcp_mount.py = 13 passed.

- [x] D14.19 `POST /decks/synergy` — pairwise co-occurrence signal. New endpoint in `src/biblioplex/routers/decks.py` (`SynergyRequest{decklist}`, `?limit=[1,100]` default 25). ONE `text()` SQL with 3 CTEs: `tag_pairs` (`oracle_tags a ⋈ oracle_tags b ON a.tag = b.tag AND a.oracle_id < b.oracle_id`, gated on `deck_oids` both sides), `combo_pairs` (LATERAL unnest of `combos.oracle_ids` as `(ea, eb)` with `ea.oid < eb.oid`, gated on `deck_oids` both sides), `pair_scores` (FULL OUTER JOIN of the two, `score = n_tags + 5 * n_combos`). Order `score DESC, a_name ASC, b_name ASC` for deterministic ties. Bug.27 rule respected — `CAST(:deck_oids AS uuid[])` throughout, no `::uuid[]`. Response `{object: "deck_synergy", pairs: [{a: {id, name}, b: {id, name}, score, shared_tags, shared_combos}], unresolved}`. Empty on stock deployments without a completed oracle_tags OR combos ingest — no schema delta. MCP tool `synergy_decklist` auto-registered. Pins: 8 new in `tests/test_decks_synergy.py` (envelope, pair shape, combo weight = 5x, SQL binds `deck_oids` + `limit`, Bug.27 `CAST` not `::`, empty-resolve skips SQL, limit clamp 422, empty body 422). Gate: pytest tests/test_decks_synergy.py + tests/test_mcp_mount.py = 13 passed.

- [x] D14.17 `POST /decks/goldfish?turns=6` — deterministic solitaire sim. New pure-fn `src/biblioplex/decklist/goldfish.py` (`GoldfishStats`, `run_goldfish(pool, *, sims, turns, hand_size, rng, keep_land_min=2, keep_land_max=5, max_mulligans=3)`) and endpoint in `routers/decks.py` reusing D14.10's mulligan pool builder + `_is_land`. Per-sim rules: mulligan on non-keep, then per turn draw one → play a land if in hand → cast biggest-first spells that fit `mana = lands_in_play`. Averages per-turn mana / spells cast / lands in play across `sims` runs; `sample_lines` (one per turn) taken from the first sim under the seed for deterministic spot-checking. Non-goals: interaction, targeting, colored mana, mulligan-to-4 EV, activated abilities. `hands` clamped `[10, 5000]`, `turns` `[1, 12]`, `hand_size` `[1, 20]`. MCP tool `goldfish_decklist` auto-registered. Pins: 9 new in `tests/test_decks_goldfish.py` (6 pure-fn: empty-pool zero-stats, hand > pool zero-stats, deterministic-under-seed, per-turn arrays match `turns` length, avg lands monotonic non-decreasing, sample_lines one-per-turn; 3 endpoint: envelope, empty 422, oversize 422). ### M14 · Deckbuilder power

- [x] D14.10 `POST /decks/mulligan` — Monte-Carlo opening-hand sampler. New pure module `src/biblioplex/decklist/mulligan.py` with `HandStats` frozen dataclass + `sample_hands(pool, *, n_hands, hand_size, rng, keep_land_min=2, keep_land_max=5) -> HandStats`. `pool` is a flat list of `(name, cmc, is_land)` tuples — one per copy (a 4-of contributes 4 tuples). `keep_rate = fraction of hands with keep_land_min ≤ lands ≤ keep_land_max` (London-mulligan heuristic; no formal EV, no bottom-decking sim). Deterministic when caller passes a seeded `random.Random`; module never reads the wall clock. Sentinel path: empty pool / non-positive counts / `hand_size` > pool → `_zero_stats(hand_size)` — `hands=0` + zeros so downstream code needn't branch. `statistics.median/mean` over Python ints is fine at 500 hands (<100 ms even at 10k). New pydantic `MulliganRequest` with `Field(default=..., ge=..., le=...)` clamps `hands` ∈ [1, 10000], `hand_size` ∈ [1, 20], and threads optional `seed`. Router `decklist_mulligan` in `routers/decks.py`: reuse `parse_decklist` + `resolve_and_fetch_deck_rows`, build the pool via the existing `_is_land` layout-aware detector (MDFC front-face awareness free from D13.2), coerce `cmc` via `int(float(cmc_val))` with defensive fallback to `None` on non-numeric X/*, then hand off to `sample_hands`. Response `{object: "deck_mulligan", hands, hand_size, seed, pool_size, stats: {land_min, land_max, land_median, land_mean, cmc_le_3_mean, keep_rate}, unresolved}` — floats rounded to 4dp at the router seam so the wire shape is stable. Surfaces: MCP tool `decklist_mulligan` auto-registered via D17.1; pinned in `test_mcp_mount.py` expected list. Web UI: new `Mulligan` button in the Decklist tab's action row (index.html) + handler in app.js.template that POSTs `hands=500, hand_size=7, seed=null` (each click reshuffles) and renders a `<section class="mulligan-stats">` with keep-rate (as %), land min/median/max, land mean, cmc≤3 mean, and an unresolved-list footer when any. tech_spec.md gains §6.15 with pool-construction rules + response envelope + sentinel path; README "Endpoints" gains `/decks/mulligan` block with curl. Pins in new `tests/test_mulligan.py` (12 pure-fn tests: all-lands 0% keep, all-spells 0% keep, balanced deck reasonable keep-rate bounds 0.5..0.9, seed reproducibility, distinct seeds diverge, empty pool → zero-stats, hand_size>pool → zero-stats, zero hands → zero-stats, cheap-only pool cmc_le_3_mean = hand_size, high-CMC pool cmc_le_3_mean = 0, land_median between min & max, HandStats dataclass shape pin) + `tests/test_decks_mulligan.py` (9 router tests: envelope + pool_size + hand_size + seed round-trip, seed determinism across two POSTs, empty/oversize decklist → 422, all-lands → keep=0 + land_min=7, unresolved surfaces, `hands` low+high out of range → 422, `hand_size` out of range → 422, 4 Forest → pool_size=4). Gate: ruff clean on touched files (3 pre-existing errors unchanged elsewhere), mypy clean (46 src files, +1 for new pure module), pytest 1013 → 1034 pass (+21 new: 12 pure-fn + 9 router; 1 pre-existing Bug.28 hypothesis failure unchanged).

- [x] D14.8 `GET /cards/:id/related` — resolve `raw.all_parts` (Scryfall's per-printing related-card list) through our name index so consumers get canonical card ids for the collapsed printings we serve. Single-statement LEFT JOIN LATERAL over `jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(c.all_parts, '[]'::jsonb)) WITH ORDINALITY part_ord(part, ord)` → LEFT JOIN LATERAL a per-part `LOWER(rc.name) = LOWER(part_ord.part->>'name') LIMIT 1` name resolve. Response `{object: "list", data: [{component, name, type_line, card: <raw | null>}]}` — `card: null` when the referenced name isn't in our corpus (unimplemented token, deleted printing) so agents can still render "combo piece: <name>". Cached with the same weak-ETag envelope as `/cards/:id/tags` keyed on `oracle_id`. Response `{object: "deck_upgrades", swaps: [{out: {id, name, edhrec_rank}, in: <decorated raw>, matched_label, score}], commander_color_identity, unresolved}` — `_decorate()` reused from `routers.cards` to attach `predicted_labels` + `label_scores` to the `in` payload matching /suggest's shape. `budget_usd` validated `>0` at the pydantic layer; `limit` clamped to `[1, 25]` via FastAPI Query. Step 8 (Smoke) skipped — feature is a no-op when the combos table is empty (default deployment); smoke stack has no `COMBOS_SOURCE_URL` set.

- [x] D14.2 `combo:` DSL operator + `GET /cards/:id/combos`. New `_COMBO_KEYS = frozenset({"combo"})` alongside `_OTAG_KEYS` in `dsl/compiler.py`; `_compile_combo` mirrors `_compile_otag`'s EXISTS shape but joins the `combos` side table on `cards.oracle_id = ANY(combos.oracle_ids)` (backed by the D14.1 `combos_oracle_ids_gin` index). Two DSL forms: `combo:has` (sentinel, case-insensitive) matches any combo membership; `combo:<slug>` matches a specific combo id. Non-`:` operators (`combo=foo`, `combo>foo`, …) surface as `unsupported_operator` 422 at the router. New `GET /cards/:id/combos` endpoint beside `/cards/:id/tags` — one LEFT-JOIN query with a LATERAL name-resolution subquery (`unnest(oracle_ids) WITH ORDINALITY → JOIN cards`) so the response surfaces card names (nicer for direct consumers) rather than raw uuids. Scryfall-shaped list envelope; empty data when the card exists but no combo references it (e.g. stock deployment with `COMBOS_SOURCE_URL` unset); 404 on unknown card; 422 on non-UUID path. Response cached with the same weak-ETag shape as `/cards/:id/tags`, keyed on `oracle_id`. Tests: 11 DSL cases (`combo:has` → any-membership, `combo:<slug>` → id + membership, slug lowercased, sentinel case-insensitive, combines with `c:r`, parametrized reject over 6 non-`:` ops) + 6 router cases (single hit, multiple combos, empty when card has no combos, 404 on unknown, 422 on bad UUID, null `card_names` defensively → empty list). README "DSL reference" gains 2 lines; new "Endpoints → GET /cards/:id/combos" block. Gate: ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest 739 → 756 pass (1 skipped, +17 new). Step 8 (Smoke) skipped — endpoint returns empty data when combos are absent (default deployment); smoke stack has no combos ingested.

- [x] D14.1 Combos ingest — Commander Spellbook. New optional data source, disabled by default (empty `COMBOS_SOURCE_URL`). Schema: `combos (id text PK, oracle_ids uuid[], color_identity text[], prereqs text, steps text, results text[])` + two GIN indexes on `oracle_ids` and `color_identity`, all `IF NOT EXISTS` so existing deployments can apply in place. New `src/biblioplex/ingest/combos.py` with pure `build_rows(combos, name_to_oracle)` helper (drops combos with any unresolved card, dedups by id, normalizes `color_identity` from either string or list to canonical WUBRG order, normalizes `results` from str/list/None to `list[str]`), `resolve_names_to_oracle_ids(engine, names)` batched DB helper (one `WHERE LOWER(name) = ANY(:lowered)`), `fetch_combo_bulk(client, url)` accepting either bare array or `{"combos": [...]}` shape, and `ingest_combos_from_url(engine, url)` orchestrator (truncate + batched INSERT in one txn; short-circuits to 0 when URL is empty). Refresh runner gains a `combos` param + `_noop_combos` default + a new Txn 5 after the oracle-tags block; failure is logged and swallowed identically to the tags branch so a third-party JSON source can't strand the cards refresh. README "Scryfall data sources" gains a "Commander Spellbook combos — opt-in" bullet with the expected JSON shape + in-place ALTER recipe for existing deployments; env table gains the `COMBOS_SOURCE_URL` row.
